== 6. 两个关键机制（能省你好几天，务必先看） ==
(a) definability 处理不了"部分应用"时，**不是数学问题，是缺实例**。
    失败形态：⊢ {Γ:=𝚺, rank:=1}.DefinableFunction fun v => shiftVec b (v 78)
    （aesop 撞规则深度上限 30/50 而失败）。
    解法：给函数**同时**登记 𝚺ᴬ₁ 版与**层级版** Γᴬ_[m+1]-Function₂ 实例 ——
    Foundation 自己的 disjSeqSubst（Formula/Iteration.lean:211）就是这么做的。
    补上后 3 秒通过。对 iVE、iT 同样适用。
(b) semiformula_result_induction 的结论把**输出层 param** 与**输入层 n** 独立量化：
      ∀ {param n p}, IsSemiformula L n p → P param n p (c.result L param p)
    而 iTR param … 要求 v 在层 param，hRel 只给 IsSemitermVec ℒₒᵣ k n v（层 n）。
    param ≠ n 时证不出来。**出路**：归纳谓词取
      P param n p q := param = n → IsSemiformula ℒₛₑₜ param q
    结论实例化 param := n 即得目标；hall 支的递归是 P (param+1) (n+1) …，护罩自动保持。
    （对照：Foundation 的 semiformula_subst_induction 不需要护罩，因为 subst 不改层；iT 改层所以要。）
(c) 小机制：ISigma1.sigma1_succ_induction 名义上要 𝗜𝚺⁺₁，但 ISigma_equiv_IBroadSigma 1
    有实例（Induction/Equiv.lean:232），现有 [V ⊧ 𝗜𝚺₁] 就够。
    Sentence.coe_quote_eq_quote : (↑(⌜σ⌝ : ℕ) : V) = ⌜σ⌝（Formula/Coding.lean:440）——
    cRelN/cDomN/cFuncN 都是 ℕ 层引号，每条公式性引理都要先 rw 它。

== 8. 陷阱 ==
- omega 在 V（ORingStructure V）上**不工作**。算术不等式用 lt_add_self_add_one（PeanoMinus.lean:14）；ring 可用。
- ↑(n+1) : V 的层级转换要 push_cast；(b+n)+1 vs b+(n+1) 要 add_assoc。
- Semiformula.quote_isSemiformula 只收 Semiproposition，要用 Sentence.quote_isSemiformula。
- simp only [cFunc, cFuncN, reduceIte] 约化 0 = 0 但**不**约化 1 = 0 / 2 = 0；
  后者要显式 split，把不可能支用 absurd h (by decide) 关掉。
- if_neg 已弃用（用 ite_eq_right）。
- InternalTranslate.lean:212 把 shiftVecDef/cFuncDef/conjunctDef/iVEDef/iTRDef 等标了 irreducible；
  definability 靠实例而不是展开它们。
- 模块文档注释必须在 import 之后。noncomputable 传染。Jeroslow.lean 不在本地导入闭包里。
- 别信旧 worktree 的 git status 的 "M"：基线是旧提交时，M 里可能既有"其实没变"的，
  也有"dev 反而更新"的。必须逐个 diff 再逐字插入。

== 9. 已排除的路（别重复） ==
第一轮交接列的（仍有效）：
 1) Sh.Δ₁ 取 ch := trProv（ProperOn 要求 trProv ↔ ¬trProv，假）
 2) 给 Provable 𝗭𝗙𝗜 x 找有界形式 ∃ d < x（对 Sh 这样的界不存在，否则 Sh 可判定）
 3) provable_D3 (T := Sh)（需 Sh.Δ₁，循环；正解是 T := Sh.craig）
 4) T := Sh.craig 直接套 provable_internalize（理论不对，还要翻译回 ZFC，更难）
 5) 把 Provable 𝗭𝗙𝗜 ⌜σᵗ⌝ (V) 当元层 𝗭𝗙𝗜 ⊢ σᵗ 用（只在 ℕ 层成立）
本轮新排除的（← 方向的四条捷径，已读码确认）：
 6) DirectInterpretation.of_provability —— 它是**语义**证明（走 complete_on_eq_models），
    不产生非标准 V 里的证明编码
 7) Derivable.of_ss —— **只在同一语言内**（Δ₁Class ⊆ Δ₁Class），ℒₒᵣ → ℒₛₑₜ 跨不过去
 8) Derivation.sound —— **只对 d : ℕ（标准推导）**&#8203;成立
 9) Diagonalization 𝗭𝗙𝗖 岔路 —— Provability T₀ T 要求 T₀ ⪯ T 是**同语言**关系，
    𝗜𝚺₁ ⪯ 𝗭𝗙𝗖 类型不过；且 Foundation 的 provabilityPred 是算术的（Semisentence ℒₒᵣ 1）

## 结论：D3 攻坚第 2 层的「公式保持性地基」已经落地并出了正式收据（CG001-C-391）

这一轮我把接手文档 §5 清单里的 [1]–[5] 全部证完，`zfcTr_D3_internalize` 仍未证，但它前面的整块地基现在有了机器证据。

**新收据（两条判据运行都精确重放）**

- 主运行 `HoTT/verification/runs/20261011-CG001-Z0-W8-LAYER2-ISEMIFORMULA-IT-01`：31 个源文件、553 个外部依赖 pin 全部校验通过，exit 0、stderr 0 B，`#print axioms` 全为 `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`，`--rerun` → `PASS_WITH_SCOPE / EXACT_EXIT_STDOUT_STDERR_MATCH`。
- 负控制 `…-NEG-02`：把 `iVE` 的 bvar 计算规则错写成「层数不加一」后，内核在 `eqAtom ^#(0+1) = eqAtom ^#0` 处拒绝，`--rerun --expect-rejected` → `NEGATIVE_CONTROL_REJECTED_AS_EXPECTED / EXACT_EXIT_STDOUT_STDERR_MATCH`。
- `…-NEG-01` 是驱动修正期（源清单漏了 `SeqTranslateFormula.lean`，拒绝发生在缺 olean 而不是预期目标），按仓库惯例保留为失败谱系、不淘汰，判据以 `-NEG-02` 为准。

**证了什么（都在 `GodelQ/ZFC/SeqTranslateFormula.lean`，任意 `[V↓[ℒₒᵣ] ⊧* 𝗜𝚺₁]` 的模型 V 中）**

1. `iVE` 的三条计算规则：`iVE_bvar`、`iVE_fvar`、`iVE_func` —— 从 `iVE.construction` 的 fixpoint 语义里抠出来，此前只有可定义性、没有任何化简。
2. `isSemiformula_iVE : IsSemiterm ℒₒᵣ n t → IsSemiformula ℒₛₑₜ (n+1) (iVE n t)` —— 项侧确实没有带层数的 TermRec 归纳，正确工具是 `IsSemiterm.sigma1_induction`；`definability` 义务需要给 `iVE` 补层级实例 `Γᴬ_[m+1]-Function₂`（照 `shiftVec` 的先例）。
3. `isSemiformula_domZero`、4. `isSemiformula_iTR`。
5. **公式保持性主定理** `isSemiformula_iT : IsSemiformula ℒₒᵣ n p → IsSemiformula ℒₛₑₜ n (iT n p)`。归纳谓词用 `P param n p q := param = n → IsSemiformula ℒₛₑₜ param q` 护罩——`semiformula_result_induction` 把输出层 `param` 与输入层 `n` 独立量化，而 `iT` 改层，护罩是必需的；`hall`/`hexs` 支用 `result_all`/`result_exs` + `nth_adjoin_zero` 取结果向量第 0 项。

**诚实的边界**：本层是第二层 10 个 case 组装（`Derivation.*` 每个构造子都要求 `IsFormulaSet`）的公共前置，不证 10 个 case，不证 `iT` 与 `subst/shift/free/setShift/emb` 的相容，不证 `axm` 支与 `codeOfREPred` 一般 V 表示引理，不证 `keyE`、`bridge`、`zfcTr_D3_internalize`。Z0 完整形式仍以 `zfcTr_D3_internalize` 为显式前提；C-120 的影子形式不受影响。

**账目**：证据索引 §41、共享矩阵 `W8-L2` 节、`形式化追踪` 02 节/总览/更新日志、CG-007 的 `LOG.md`/`STATE.json`、`MEMORY/001` 都已写回；提交 `6fed2520`（形式化 + 收据）、`e7beba9b`（修正被 zsh 吞掉的反引号标识符的提交信息）、`d085ff6e`（goal-local 校验输出）、`7452752f`（MEMORY）、`917d411d`（dev-notes 归档）。

**与并行会话的关系**：我开工时发现另一个会话（Codex 9276 续做）正在同一主检出里跑它的收据。它做 C-388（第 1 层七条交换律）与 C-390（宇宙多态桥 + `bwd` 归约为一条消去 `ShCraigElim`），我做 C-391（第 2 层地基），互补不重叠；C-390 已由它在 `7d6ad033` 入库，C-388 与其收据仍在工作区未提交，我没有碰。它在我抓收据期间把我追加到共享文件的行一并带进了它的提交，我已核对工作区与 HEAD 一致。

**下一步（最小可验单点）**：`derivable_translateSeq` 的 10 个 case 组装。施工图 `/tmp/d3build/src/.../SeqTranslateDerivable.lean` 从未编译过，10 个 `Derivation.*` 构造子调用是猜测，`translateSeq_isFormulaSet`、`fstIdx_axL` 等辅助引理不存在——先搬进包，再逐条修；C-391 已经把每个 case 都要用的 `IsFormulaSet` 地基铺好。

## Z0 D3 的形式化目标链（现在站在哪）

要落地的目标是 Z0 完整形式，读作 `𝗭𝗙𝗖 ⊬ (𝗭𝗙𝗖.consistent)ᵗ`（即 `𝗭𝗙𝗜 ⊬ Con(𝗭𝗙𝗜)`）。

- **已无条件成立**：影子形式 `𝗭𝗙𝗖 ⊬ (Sh.craig.consistent)ᵗ`（C-120），不受本缺口影响。
- **缺口已收缩成一条句法等价**：`zfcTr_D3_internalize : 𝗜𝚺₁ ⊢ zfcTr σ 🡘 provabilityPred Sh.craig σ`。
  - `→` 方向有清楚路线（V 内部 Δ₁ 机器：Foundation 的 `Derivable.of_ss` + `provable_standardProvability_imp_of_Δ₁Class_subset` + `Theory.reWitness_spec`/`mem_craig_codes_iff`，可选并行支线）。
  - `←` 方向 = **V 内部的语法解释定理**（ℒₒᵣ → ℒₛₑₜ 的推导翻译）。这是核心难点，也是 `zfcTr_D3_internalize` 的精确内容（见 `GodelQ/ZFC/Z0Blocked.lean` 的 `sorry` 命题）。
- **入口障碍已清除**（C-390）：宇宙多态桥 + `bwd` 被归约为**一条消去 `ShCraigElim`**。所以现在的硬骨头就是证出 `ShCraigElim`，而 `zfcTr_D3_internalize` 是它（接上桥后）的推论。
- **第 2 层地基已落地并出收据**（CG001-C-391，提交 `6fed2520`）：`iVE` 三条计算规则、`isSemiformula_iVE`/`domZero`/`iTR`、公式保持性主定理 `isSemiformula_iT`。这正是 `Derivation.induction1`（10 条规则支）每个 case 都需要的 `IsFormulaSet` 地基。`InternalTranslate.lean` 还已躺好 `iT_rel/nrel/verum/falsum/and/or/all/exs` 与 `iT_quote`。

一句话价值定位：第 2 层里**数学上最重的部分（公式保持性）已经证完并精确重放**，缺口从"形式化 Σ₁ 完全性"缩成"一条 V 内部的语法推导翻译定理"，路径是具体的、可逐 case 验收的，不是再开一个研究赌局。

## 后续路线图（按 [6]–[10]，每条都可独立验收）

剩余工作 = 把 `ShCraigElim`（V 内部 Craig 句推导翻译定理）这个 10 规则归纳做满，再接回 `keyE`/`zfcTr_D3_internalize`。顺序按依赖排：

1. **`iT` 与 `substs1`/`shift`/`free`/`setShift` 的相容**（规格 §5 第 6 项）。
   - 这是 `all`/`shift`/`exs`/`setShift` 支要用的。翻译每个量词多引入一个约束变量，de Bruijn 结构变了，**不是平凡交换**，需要用 `IsSemiformula.qqAlls` + 第 2 层已证的引理逐条构造。
   - 验证：在 `SeqTranslateFormula.lean` 或新建 `SeqTranslateCompat.lean`，每条配一个负控制（改错结论 → 内核拒绝）。

2. **`axm` 支（规格 §5 第 7 项）**：唯一用到 `𝗭𝗙𝗖` 的地方。
   - 缺的是 `codeOfREPred` 的**一般 V 表示引理**——Foundation 的 `codeOfREPred_spec` 只在 ℕ 层。
   - 拿到后：`mem_craig_codes_iff` + `reWitness_spec` ⇒ `zfcTr ρ`（V 手里已有的 `ρᵗ` 的 ZFC 推导），再用 `ρ.padding s = ρ ⋏ ⊤ ⋏ …` 接回。
   - 这一步要先确认 Foundation 能提供 `Σ₁`-class 的 V 层表示，否则把"缺什么"精确写回 `Z0Blocked.lean`。

3. **`derivable_translateSeq` 的 10 个 case 组装**（规格 §5 第 8 项，依赖上面 1–2）。
   - `/tmp/d3build/src/.../SeqTranslateDerivable.lean` 是**施工图、从未编译过**：构造子调用是猜的，辅助引理（`translateSeq_isFormulaSet`、`fstIdx_axL` 等）不存在。先搬进包，再逐条修，**别当既有成果用**。
   - 每个 case 都吃第 2 层的主定理 `isSemiformula_iT`（已证）。

4. **`bridge` / `keyE` 的 `←` / `zfcTr_D3_internalize`**（规格 §5 第 9 项）。
   - `ShCraigElim` 落地后接 C-390 的宇宙多态桥，推出 `keyE` 的 `←`，即得 `Z0Full.zfc_z0_full_of_D3_internalize` 的前件，`zfcTr_D3_internalize` 闭合。
   - 注意：`Foundation` 的 `of_provability` 是语义证明（走 `complete_on_eq_models`），不产生非标准 V 里的证明编码——这正是 §9 已排除的捷径，别回头走。

5. **正式收据**（规格 §5 第 10 项）：用 `.claude/goals/CG-007-formalization-completion/tools/capture_run.py`（带沙箱，拒 `sorry`）。
   - 开发迭代回路用 `python3 -B .claude/goals/CG-007-formalization-completion/tools/devbuild.py <PKG> <按 import 拓扑序的模块…>`，缓存落仓库内 `.cg007-build/`。
   - 每个新收据满足 F-011 五件事（源落 `.lean`、主运行 + 负控制抓进 `HoTT/verification/runs/`、先写证据索引行再 `verify_cg001_run.py --rerun`、矩阵追加一节、更新 `形式化追踪/`）。

```lean
import GodelQ.ZFC.SeqTranslateFormula

open FFL FFL.FirstOrder FFL.FirstOrder.Arithmetic FFL.FirstOrder.SetTheory Bootstrapping
open scoped FFL.FirstOrder.Arithmetic FFL.FirstOrder.Bounding
open GodelQ.ZFCArith GodelQ.ZFCZ0 GodelQ.Translate
open GodelQ.SeqTranslate

namespace GodelQ.ShCraigElimNegLaw

variable {V : Type*} [ORingStructure V] [V↓[ℒₒᵣ] ⊧* 𝗜𝚺₁]

/-- 一般（V 内任意公式码）的 `iT` 与 `neg` 相容律。
这是 `hAxL`/`hCut`/`hShift` 三支的公共前置。 -/
theorem iT_neg {n : V} : ∀ p, IsSemiformula ℒₒᵣ n p →
    iT n (neg ℒₒᵣ p) = neg ℒₛₑₜ (iT n p) := by
  have key : ∀ {param n p : V}, IsSemiformula ℒₒᵣ n p →
      iT param (neg ℒₒᵣ p) = neg ℒₛₑₜ (iT.construction.result ℒₒᵣ param p) :=
    UformulaRec1.Construction.semiformula_result_induction
      (V := V) (c := iT.construction) (L := ℒₒᵣ)
      (P := fun param _n p q => iT param (neg ℒₒᵣ p) = neg ℒₛₑₜ q)
      (by definability)
      (by intro n param k R v hR hv; simp [iT_rel hR hv, iT_nrel hR hv, neg_rel (L := ℒₛₑₜ) hR'? , ...])
      ...
  · intro p hp
    simpa [iT] using key hp
```

### Corrected file content (overwrite the existing file)
```lean
import GodelQ.ZFC.SeqTranslateFormula

open FFL FFL.FirstOrder FFL.FirstOrder.Arithmetic FFL.FirstOrder.SetTheory Bootstrapping
open scoped FFL.FirstOrder.Arithmetic FFL.FirstOrder.Bounding
open GodelQ.ZFCArith GodelQ.ZFCZ0 GodelQ.Translate
open GodelQ.SeqTranslate

namespace GodelQ.ShCraigElimNegLaw

variable {V : Type*} [ORingStructure V] [V↓[ℒₒᵣ] ⊧* 𝗜𝚺₁]

theorem iT_neg {n : V} : ∀ p, IsSemiformula ℒₒᵣ n p →
    iT n (neg ℒₒᵣ p) = neg ℒₛₑₜ (iT n p) := by
  have key : ∀ {param n p : V}, IsSemiformula ℒₒᵣ n p →
      iT param (neg ℒₒᵣ p) = neg ℒₛₑₜ (iT.construction.result ℒₒᵣ param p) :=
    UformulaRec1.Construction.semiformula_result_induction
      (V := V) (c := iT.construction) (L := ℒₒᵣ)
      (P := fun param _n p q => iT param (neg ℒₒᵣ p) = neg ℒₛₑₜ q)
      (by definability)
      (by intro n param k R v hR hv
        simp [iT.construction]
        rw [neg_rel (L := ℒₒᵣ) hR hv, iT_nrel hR hv]; rfl)
      (by intro n param k R v hR hv
        simp [iT.construction]
        rw [neg_nrel (L := ℒₒᵣ) hR hv, iT_rel hR hv,
          IsUFormula.neg_neg (isSemiformula_iTR hR hv).isUFormula]; rfl)
      (by intro n param
        simp [iT.construction]
        rw [neg_verum (L := ℒₒᵣ), iT_falsum, neg_verum (L := ℒₛₑₜ)]; rfl)
      (by intro n param
        simp [iT.construction]
        rw [neg_falsum (L := ℒₒᵣ), iT_verum, neg_falsum (L := ℒₛₑₜ)]; rfl)
      (by intro n param p q hp hq _ihp _ihq
        have hsp := (isSemiformula_iT p hp).isUFormula
        have hsq := (isSemiformula_iT q hq).isUFormula
        simp [iT.construction]
        rw [neg_and (L := ℒₒᵣ) hp.isUFormula hq.isUFormula,
          iT_or hp.isUFormula.neg hq.isUFormula.neg, ← iT_neg p hp, ← iT_neg q hq,
          neg_and (L := ℒₛₑₜ) hsp hsq, iT_and hp.isUFormula hq.isUFormula]; rfl)
      (by intro n param p q hp hq _ihp _ihq
        have hsp := (isSemiformula_iT p hp).isUFormula
        have hsq := (isSemiformula_iT q hq).isUFormula
        simp [iT.construction]
        rw [neg_or (L := ℒₒᵣ) hp.isUFormula hq.isUFormula,
          iT_and hp.isUFormula.neg hq.isUFormula.neg, ← iT_neg p hp, ← iT_neg q hq,
          neg_or (L := ℒₛₑₜ) hsp hsq, iT_or hp.isUFormula hq.isUFormula]; rfl)
      (by intro n param p hp _ih
        have hsp := (isSemiformula_iT p hp).isUFormula
        simp [iT.construction]
        rw [neg_all (L := ℒₒᵣ) hp.isUFormula, iT_exs hp.isUFormula.neg,
          ← iT_neg p hp,
          neg_ex (L := ℒₛₑₜ) (IsUFormula.imp (isSemiformula_domZero (n := n + 1)).isUFormula hsp),
          imp, neg_or (L := ℒₛₑₜ),
          IsUFormula.neg_neg (isSemiformula_domZero (n := n + 1)).isUFormula]; rfl)
      (by intro n param p hp _ih
        have hsp := (isSemiformula_iT p hp).isUFormula
        simp [iT.construction]
        rw [neg_ex (L := ℒₒᵣ) hp.isUFormula, iT_all hp.isUFormula.neg,
          ← iT_neg p hp,
          neg_all (L := ℒₛₑₜ) (IsUFormula.imp (isSemiformula_domZero (n := n + 1)).isUFormula hsp),
          neg_and (L := ℒₛₑₜ) (isSemiformula_domZero (n := n + 1)).isUFormula hsp,
          imp]; rfl)
  · intro p hp
    simpa [iT] using key hp

end GodelQ.ShCraigElimNegLaw

#print axioms GodelQ.ShCraigElimNegLaw.iT_neg
```
Note: in the `all` branch there must be only ONE `imp` (the one right after `neg_ex`); do not leave the spurious trailing `imp` that the broken draft had. Compile and iterate — `neg` involution at code level comes from `IsUFormula.neg_neg` (Foundation `Formula/Functions.lean:127`), valid because `iTR`/`domZero`/`iT(..)` are all `IsUFormula`.

## Key API facts (Foundation, at `/Volumes/D/HoTT-toolchain-cache/foundation-src`, commit `1fb01b72`)
- `UformulaRec1.Construction.semiformula_result_induction {P : V→V→V→V→Prop} (hP : 𝚺ᴬ₁-Relation₄ P) (hRel)(hNRel)(hverum)(hfalsum)(hand)(hor)(hall)(hexs) : ∀ {param n p}, IsSemiformula L n p → P param n p (c.result L param p)`.
- `neg_rel`/`neg_nrel`/`neg_verum`/`neg_falsum`/`neg_and`/`neg_or`/`neg_all`/`neg_ex` are `@[simp]` in `Formula/Functions.lean`; `neg_all : neg L (^∀ p) = ^∃ (neg L p)`, `neg_ex : neg L (^∃ p) = ^∀ (neg L p)`.
- `IsUFormula.neg {p}` and `IsUFormula.neg_neg {p} : IsUFormula L p → neg L (neg L p) = p` (`Formula/Functions.lean:102,127`).
- `IsUFormula.imp {p q} : IsUFormula L (imp L p q) ↔ IsUFormula L p ∧ IsUFormula L q` (line 185) — usable as explicit proof.
- `imp L a b = neg L a ⋎ b` (definition).
- `iT_rel`/`iT_nrel`/`iT_verum`/`iT_falsum`/`iT_and`/`iT_or`/`iT_all`/`iT_exs` in `InternalTranslate.lean`. `iT_all : iT n (^∀ p) = ^∀ (imp ℒₛₑₜ domZero (iT (n+1) p))`; `iT_exs : iT n (^∃ p) = ^∃ (domZero ^⋏ iT (n+1) p)`.
- `isSemiformula_iTR`, `isSemiformula_domZero`, `isSemiformula_iT` available in `GodelQ.SeqTranslate` (namespace `GodelQ.SeqTranslate`).
- `iT.construction.rel param k R v = iTR param k R v`, `iT.construction.verum param = ^⊤`, etc.

## Roadmap — what remains after `iT_neg` compiles
1. **`iT_neg`** (this file): finish as above. Unblocks `hAxL`, `hCut`, `hShift`.
2. **`iT`/`imp` law** (spec #2): `iT n (imp ℒₒᵣ p q) = imp ℒₛₑₜ (iT n p) (iT n q)` — analogous induction; needed by `cut`/`hCut`.
3. **`iT` vs `shift`** (spec #3): `iT n (shift p) = shift (iT n p)` (translation changes de Bruijn by adding a bounded var → not trivial). For `all`/`shift` branches.
4. **`iT` vs `substs1`** (spec #4): `iT (n+1) (substs1 t p) = substs1 (iT 0 t) (iT (n+1) p)` — `exs` branch.
5. **`iT` vs `free`** (spec #5): `free (iT p) = iT (free p)` — `all` branch.
6. **`axm` branch** (spec #6): unique place using `𝗭𝗙𝗖`. Needs `codeOfREPred` general-V representation lemma (`codeOfREPred_spec` only at ℕ layer). Then `mem_craig_codes_iff` + `reWitness_spec` ⇒ `zfcTr ρ`.

持续推进工作，直至完成全部的形式化与机器证明

Continuation behavior:
- This goal persists across turns. Ending this turn does not require shrinking the objective to what fits now.
- Keep the full objective intact. If it cannot be finished now, make concrete progress toward the real requested end state, leave the goal active, and do not redefine success around a smaller or easier task.
- Temporary rough edges are acceptable while the work is moving in the right direction. Completion still requires the requested end state to be true and verified.

Budget:

- Token budget: none
- Tokens remaining: unbounded

Work from evidence:
Use the current worktree and external state as authoritative. Previous conversation context can help locate relevant work, but inspect the current state before relying on it. Improve, replace, or remove existing work as needed to satisfy the actual objective.

No-progress check:
- Classify the previous goal turn as progress, a verified wait, or no progress. Progress changes authoritative state, completes work, or yields evidence that changes the next action; status restatements and unexecuted plans are no progress.
- A verified wait polls a specific process, session, job, or tool handle confirmed live now. Conversation, intent, prior output, or a lock or state file alone is insufficient. Treat work as stopped only when authoritative state says it is terminal or its handle is missing. An observation timeout or transient polling failure is not terminal: re-poll the same handle or inspect other authoritative state; never restart solely because observation expired.
- Revalidate a no-progress turn and take the next available safe action. If none exists because the same genuine blocker remains, report it and leave the goal active until the blocked audit threshold is met. Treat equivalent blockers as the same condition across turns even when their wording or stated next step changes.

Fidelity:
- Optimize each turn for movement toward the requested end state, not for the smallest stable-looking subset or easiest passing change.
- Do not substitute a narrower, safer, smaller, merely compatible, or easier-to-test solution because it is more likely to pass current tests.
- Treat alignment as movement toward the requested end state. An edit is aligned only if it makes the requested final state more true; useful-looking behavior that preserves a different end state is misaligned.

Completion audit:
Before deciding that the goal is achieved, treat completion as unproven and verify it against the actual current state:
- Derive concrete requirements from the objective and any referenced files, plans, specifications, issues, or user instructions.
- Preserve the original scope; do not redefine success around the work that already exists.
- For every explicit requirement, numbered item, named artifact, command, test, gate, invariant, and deliverable, identify the authoritative evidence that would prove it, then inspect the relevant current-state sources: files, command output, test results, PR state, rendered artifacts, runtime behavior, or other authoritative evidence.
- For each item, determine whether the evidence proves completion, contradicts completion, shows incomplete work, is too weak or indirect to verify completion, or is missing.
- Match the verification scope to the requirement's scope; do not use a narrow check to support a broad claim.
- Treat tests, manifests, verifiers, green checks, and search results as evidence only after confirming they cover the relevant requirement.
- Treat uncertain or indirect evidence as not achieved; gather stronger evidence or continue the work.
- The audit must prove completion, not merely fail to find obvious remaining work.

Do not rely on intent, partial progress, memory of earlier work, or a plausible final answer as proof of completion. Marking the goal complete is a claim that the full objective has been finished and can withstand requirement-by-requirement scrutiny. Only mark the goal achieved when current evidence proves every requirement has been satisfied and no required work remains. If the evidence is incomplete, weak, indirect, merely consistent with completion, or leaves any requirement missing, incomplete, or unverified, keep working instead of marking the goal complete. If the objective is achieved, call update_goal with status "complete" so usage accounting is preserved. If the achieved goal has a token budget, report the final consumed token budget to the user after update_goal succeeds.

Blocked audit:
- Do not call update_goal with status "blocked" the first time a blocker appears.
- Only use status "blocked" when the same blocking condition has repeated for at least three consecutive goal turns, counting the original/user-triggered turn and any automatic goal continuations.
- If the user resumes a goal that was previously marked "blocked", treat the resumed run as a fresh blocked audit. If the same blocking condition then repeats for at least three consecutive resumed goal turns, call update_goal with status "blocked" again.
- Use status "blocked" only when you are truly at an impasse and cannot make meaningful progress without user input or an external-state change.
- Once the blocked threshold is satisfied, do not keep reporting that you are still blocked while leaving the goal active; call update_goal with status "blocked".
- Never use status "blocked" merely because the work is hard, slow, uncertain, incomplete, or would benefit from clarification.

Call update_goal only after the completion or blocked audit passes, or when the user explicitly requests pausing this goal. For a requested pause, use status "paused", report the returned status, and stop goal work; never pause on your own initiative. Do not mark a goal complete merely because the budget is nearly exhausted or because you are stopping work.

- `iT_rel`/`iT_nrel`/`iT_verum`/`iT_falsum`/`iT_and`/`iT_or`/`iT_all`/`iT_exs` are `@[simp]` lemmas in `InternalTranslate.lean:538-566`. `iT_all n p = ^∀ (imp ℒₛₑₜ domZero (iT (n+1) p))`; `iT_exs n p = ^∃ (domZero ^⋏ iT (n+1) p)` (note `all` body uses `imp`, `exs` body uses `^⋏` = `and`).
- `iTR n k R v = qqAlls (imp ℒₛₑₜ (conjunct 2 n 0 (iVE n v.[0]) ^⋏ (conjunct 2 n 1 (iVE n v.[1]) ^⋏ ^⊤)) (subst ℒₛₑₜ ?[^#0,^#1] (cRel R))) 2`.
- `IsUFormula` structural lemmas (`Foundation/.../Formula/Functions.lean` / `Basic.lean:290+`): `@[simp] rel`, `nrel`, `verum`, `falsum`, `and`, `or`, `all`, `ex`, `imp`, `neg` (all iff or `IsUFormula.neg : IsUFormula p → IsUFormula (neg p)` — returns an `IsUFormula`, do NOT project `.isUFormula` from it), `@[simp] neg_neg : IsUFormula p → neg (neg p) = p`. `qqAlls` has `isUFormula_qqAlls`. `subst` `construction.result` has `uformula_result_induction` for `IsUFormula`.
- **CRITICAL negation fact**: `neg (imp A B)` (Foundation's `neg_imp`) equals `A ^⋏ neg B` (conjunction), **NOT** `imp A (neg B)`. This is the bug in the current `all`/`exs` branches — I wrongly rewrote `neg (imp A B)` as `imp A (neg B)`.

## 4. What's broken & the fix for `iT_neg`

The atomic (`rel`/`nrel`/`verum`/`falsum`) and `and`/`or` branches of `iT_neg` now close. The `all`/`exs` branches fail because of two mistakes:
1. Stray `rw [imp]` on a LHS that already has `domZero ^⋏ y` expanded (no `imp` form to rewrite).
2. Applying `IsUFormula.imp` as a function (it's an `↔` lemma, not a projection).
3. Wrong direction of negating an implication (`neg (imp A B) = A ^⋏ neg B`, not `imp A (neg B)`).

**Correct `all` branch sketch** (goal: `iT param (neg ℒₒᵣ (^∀ p)) = neg ℒₛₑₜ (iT.construction.result ℒₒᵣ param (^∀ p))`):
```
rw [neg_all (L := ℒₒᵣ) hp.isUFormula, iT_exs (IsUFormula.neg hp.isUFormula)]   -- LHS → ^∃ (domZero ^⋏ iT (param+1) (neg p))
rw [UformulaRec1.Construction.result_all (V := V) (c := iT.construction) (L := ℒₒᵣ) hp.isUFormula (rfl : iT.construction.allSize param (^∀ p) = 1)]  -- RHS inner → ^∀ (imp domZero (iT (param+1) p))
rw [neg_all (L := ℒₛₑₜ) (IsUFormula.all (IsUFormula.imp (isSemiformula_domZero (n := n+1)).isUFormula (isSemiformula_iT p hp).isUFormula))]  -- RHS → ^∃ (neg (imp domZero (iT (param+1) p)))
rw [neg_imp]   -- ^∃ (domZero ^⋏ neg (iT (param+1) p))   [negation of implication = AND of antecedent and negated consequent]
exact congrArg (fun x => (^∃ (domZero ^⋏ x : V))) (ih 0 (by simp) (by simp))
```
(The `exs` branch is the symmetric case using `neg_ex`, `iT_all`, `result_exs`, `neg_imp`.) Verify `neg_imp` exists in Foundation (`grep neg_imp` in `Foundation/FirstOrder/Arithmetic/Bootstrapping/Syntax/Formula/Functions.lean`); if it's named differently, find the exact lemma. The `ih` argument types/order must match `semiformula_result_induction`'s `hall`/`hexs` hypotheses: `∀ i < c.allSize param p, P (c.allChanges param i) (n+1) p (c.result L (c.allChanges param i) p)`.

## Key API facts (verified against live Foundation source)

- `iT.construction.allChanges n _ := n + 1` (NOT default `param`). Therefore `iT_neg`'s `all`/`exs` branches **require the layer-shielded predicate** `P param n p q := param = n → iT param (neg p) = neg q` (exactly like committed `isSemiformula_iT` at `SeqTranslateFormula.lean:348`). The prior unshielded `P := fun param n p q => iT param (neg p) = neg q` cannot do `all`/`exs` (layer mismatch).
- `iT_all n p = ^∀ (imp ℒₛₑₜ domZero (iT (n+1) p))`; `iT_exs n p = ^∃ (domZero ^⋏ iT (n+1) p)` (`InternalTranslate.lean:560,566`, `@[simp]`).
- `iTR n k R v = qqAlls (imp ℒₛₑₜ (conjunct 2 n 0 (iVE n v.[0]) ^⋏ (conjunct 2 n 1 (iVE n v.[1]) ^⋏ ⊤)) (subst ℒₛₑₜ ?[^#0,^#1] (cRel R))) 2`.
- `IsUFormula.neg_neg {p}` (`Functions.lean:127`, `@[simp]`): `IsUFormula L p → neg (neg p) = p` — use for the `nrel` branch via `isUFormula_iTR` (derive from committed `isSemiformula_iTR` `.isUFormula`).
- `neg_rel`/`neg_nrel`/`neg_verum`/`neg_falsum`/`neg_and`/`neg_or`/`neg_all`/`neg_ex` are `@[simp]` and need an `IsUFormula` arg.
- `isSemiformula_iTR` (`SeqTranslateFormula.lean:313`): `IsSemitermVec ℒₒᵣ k n v → IsSemiformula ℒₛₑₜ n (iTR n k R v)`. `.isUFormula` yields `IsUFormula ℒₛₑₜ (iTR n k R v)`.
- `IsSemitermVec.isUTerm` (`Basic.lean:786`): `IsSemitermVec L k n v → IsUTermVec L k v`.
- `result_all` (`Basic.lean:1142`): `c.result L param (^∀ p) = c.all param p ?[c.result L (c.allChanges param 0) p]`. For `iT.construction`, `allChanges param 0 = param + 1`.
- `semiformula_result_induction` signature (`Basic.lean:1591`): `P param n p q` with q fixed as `c.result L param p`; `hall`/`hexs` give `∀ i < allSize, P (allChanges param i) (n+1) p (result (allChanges param i) p)`.

## Codex

关键 API 已经清晰。`IsUFormula` 在 Foundation 里**没有** `qqAlls`/`imp`/`and`/`subst`/`rel` 这类"构造"引理——只有**谓词方向**的 `@[simp]`（`IsUFormula.all`/`.imp`/`.and`/`.subst`/`.rel`，这些是"若子公式满足则复合满足"）。而 `neg_*` 是真正的码层面化简（`neg ℒ (^rel ...) = ^nrel ...`，需 `IsRel`/`IsUTermVec` 或 `IsUFormula`）。

上次 `ShCraigElimNegLaw` 失败的真正原因就是用了不存在的 `IsUFormula.qqAlls`。`isUFormula_iTR` 里的 `IsUFormula.qqAlls`/`IsUFormula.imp`/`IsUFormula.and`/`IsUFormula.subst`/`IsUFormula.rel` 全都要换成对应的**谓词** `@[simp]`（如 `IsUFormula.all`/`IsUFormula.imp`/`IsUFormula.and`/`IsUFormula.subst`/`IsUFormula.rel`），而且 `iTR` 内部的 `qqAlls ... 2` 要改用 `isUFormula_qqAlls`。

## Codex

完整 API 已清楚。`IsUFormula` 的 `rel`/`nrel`/`and`/`or`/`all`/`ex` 全是**谓词等价** `@[simp]`，没有"构造"引理；`imp` 根本不是 `IsUFormula` 的成员（`imp` 定义在 `Functions.lean:169`，是 `neg p ^⋎ q`，要拆成 `neg` + `and`）。`qqAlls` 也只有 `isUFormula_qqAlls`（谓词等价）。所以上次 `ShCraigElimNegLaw` 整段 `isUFormula_iTR` 的写法（用 `IsUFormula.qqAlls`/`IsUFormula.imp`/`IsUFormula.and` 当构造引理）根本不对。

正确路线：`iT_neg` 的归纳里，`all`/`exs` 支可以直接用 `iT_all`/`iT_exs`（已在 `InternalTranslate.lean` 证为 `@[simp]`）把 `iT` 展开，再用 `neg_all`/`neg_ex`、`IsUFormula.neg_neg`、`isSemiformula_iT` 衔接——**根本不需要 `isUFormula_iTR` 去拼 iTR 内部的 `qqAlls`**。而原子 `nrel` 支只需 `iT_nrel`/`iT_rel` + `neg_nrel`/`neg_rel` + `IsUFormula.neg_neg`（作用于一个**直接给出的** `IsUFormula ℒₛₑₜ (iTR ...)`，这个可以从 `isSemiformula_iTR ... .isUFormula` 拿，但要先证明 `iTR` 是 UFormula——而 `isSemiformula_iTR` 已经给了 `IsSemiformula`，`.isUFormula` 就有）。

所以 `isUFormula_iTR` 可以**大幅简化**：直接 `exact (isSemiformula_iTR hR hv).isUFormula`（若 `IsSemiformula` 有 `.isUFormula` 字段）。但需要 `iTR n k R v` 是 UFormula，而 `isSemiformula_iTR` 给的是 `IsSemiformula ℒₛₑₜ n (iTR ...)` —— `.isUFormula` 是 `IsUFormula ℒₛₑₜ (iTR ...)`。但归纳 `nrel` 支里 `iT_nrel hR hv` 产生 `iT n (^nrel k R v)` 然后要 `neg ℒₛₑₜ` 它，需要 `IsUFormula ℒₛₑₜ (iT n (^nrel...))` —— 而 `iT` 输出是 `iTR` 形状，所以 `IsUFormula ℒₛₑₜ (iT.construction.nrel ...)` 就是 `IsUFormula ℒₛₑₜ (iTR ...)` = `(isSemiformula_iTR hR hv).isUFormula`。

## Codex

现在 `iT` 的 mechanics 完全清楚：

- `iT.construction.nrel n k R v := neg ℒₛₑₜ (iTR n k R v)` —— 所以 `iT n (^nrel k R v)`（经 nrel 支）= `neg ℒₛₑₜ (iTR n k R v)`。
- `iT.construction.rel n k R v := iTR n k R v` —— `iT n (^rel k R v)` = `iTR n k R v`。
- `iT.construction.all n ys := ^∀ (imp ℒₛₑₜ domZero ys.[0])`；`allChanges n _ := n+1`。
- `iT_all n p`/`iT_exs n p` 是 `@[simp]` 展开（InternalTranslate:560/566）。

所以 `iT_neg` 的正确版本（带 `param = n` 护罩，因 `iT` 改层，`all`/`exs` 支 `param' = n+1`）：

归纳谓词取 `P param n p q := param = n → iT param (neg ℒₒᵣ p) = neg ℒₛₑₜ q`，`q` 固定为 `iT.construction.result ℒₒᵣ param p`。

各支（在 `param = n` 假设下）：
- `rel`: `iT n (neg (^rel...))` = `iT n (^nrel...)` (neg_rel) = `neg ℒₛₑₜ (iTR n k R v)` (iT_nrel) = `neg ℒₛₑₜ (iT n (^rel...))` (iT_rel, 即 q)。✓
- `nrel`: `iT n (neg (^nrel...))` = `iT n (^rel...)` (neg_nrel) = `iTR n k R v` (iT_rel) = `neg ℒₛₑₜ (neg ℒₛₑₜ (iTR...))` ... 目标右边是 `neg ℒₛₑₜ (iT n (^nrel...))` = `neg ℒₛₑₜ (neg ℒₛₑₜ (iTR...))` (iT_nrel) = `iTR...` (IsUFormula.neg_neg on `IsUFormula ℒₛₑₜ (iTR...)`)。左边 `iTR n k R v`。所以 `iTR = iTR` ✓ 用 `IsUFormula.neg_neg`。
- `verum`: `iT n (neg ^⊤)` = `iT n ^⊥` (neg_verum) = `^⊥` (iT_falsum) = `neg ℒₛₑₜ ^⊤` (neg_falsum) = `neg ℒₛₑₜ (iT n ^⊤)` (iT_verum)。✓
- `falsum`: 对称。
- `and`: `iT n (neg (p^⋏q))` = `iT n (neg p ^⋎ neg q)` (neg_and) = `iT n (neg p) ^⋎ iT n (neg q)` (iT_or) = `neg(iT p) ^⋎ neg(iT q)` (ih) = `neg (iT p ^⋏ iT q)` (neg_or) = `neg (iT n (p^⋏q))` (iT_and)。✓
- `or`: 对称。

## 3. 修复 `isUFormula_iTR` 的真实 API（Foundation 实测）
- **存在**：`IsUFormula.neg_neg` (Functions.lean:127)、`IsUFormula.imp` (Functions.lean:185)、`IsUFormula.neg` (:102)、`IsUFormula.and/or/all/ex` 与 `rel/nrel/verum/falsum`（Basic.lean 内 `namespace IsUFormula` ≈ line 32–98，都是 ↔ iff）、`isUFormula_qqAlls` (Iteration.lean:515：`IsUFormula L (qqAlls p k) ↔ IsUFormula L p`)、`substs_rel` (Functions.lean： `subst L w (^rel k R v) = ^rel k R (termSubstVec L k w v)`)。
- **不存在**：`IsUFormula.qqAlls`、`IsUFormula.and`（作为构造子）、`IsUFormula.subst`、`IsUFormula.rel`（构造子）、`IsUFormula.cRel`。`IsUFormula` 是 `def`（fixpoint 谓词），没有 `.mpr`/`.and` 字段访问——旧代码 `IsUFormula.and.mpr` 之类全部非法。
- **最省事的修法**：`iTR` 是 layer-free 的；已编译证明的 `isSemiformula_iTR` 返回 `IsSemiformula ℒₛₑₜ n (iTR ...)`，其结构字段就是 `.isUFormula`，故可直接 `(isSemiformula_iTR hR hv).isUFormula`。注意签名差异：`isSemiformula_iTR` 要 `IsSemitermVec ℒₒᵣ k n v`，而 `iT_neg` 的 nrel 支给的是 `hv : IsUTermVec ℒₒᵣ k v`——需把 `IsUTermVec` 转成适当 `IsSemitermVec`（查 `IsUTermVec.isSemitermVec` 或类似投影）。若转换别扭，就改用 `isUFormula_qqAlls` + `IsUFormula.imp` + `IsUFormula.and` + `IsUFormula.rel` + `substs_rel` 手工拼。

## 4. `iT_neg` 关键机制（已验证）
- `semiformula_result_induction`（Basic.lean:1574）签名 `P param n p q`；`iT` 改层（all/exs 支 `allChanges = n+1`），故归纳谓词必须带 `param = n` 护罩，且 `all`/`exs` 递归的 `ih` 调用是 `ih 0 (by simp [iT.construction]) (by simp [iT.construction, h])`（索引 0，因 `allSize/exsSize = 1`）。
- `isSemiformula_iT` 是 `{n} : ∀ p, IsSemiformula ℒₒᵣ n p → ...`，调用时写 `isSemiformula_iT p hp`（让 `n` 从 `hp` 推断），**不要**写 `isSemiformula_iT (param + 1) p hp`。
- `iT.construction.all _ _ ys = ^∀ (imp ℒₛₑₜ domZero ys.[0])`，`exs = ^∃ (domZero ^⋏ ys.[0])`，`allChanges/exsChanges n _ = n+1`（InternalTranslate.lean:231–256）。

这是**宿主 App（WorkBuddy）给这个会话设的默认启动目录**，不是我的工作目录，也不是什么异常。逐项拆开看：

**`pwd` 显示的那个路径是什么**

```text
/Users/aurolafly/WorkBuddy/Worktrees/HoTT_AI_HANDOFF_20260911/dev-f65ca154
```

它是一个 **git linked worktree**，不是独立仓库：

- `.git` 是普通文件，内容 `gitdir: /Volumes/D/HoTT_AI_HANDOFF_20260911/.git/worktrees/dev-f65ca154`
- 分支 `workbuddy/dev-f65ca154`，HEAD `87b65dfd`
- 它和 `dev-4d788035`、`dev-bd4295b5`、`dev-ef7e5239` 是同一批：**四个都停在 `87b65dfd`**，是 WorkBuddy 当初开工作树时的快照

**为什么提示符是 `git:(workbuddy/dev-f65ca154) ✗`**

- `workbuddy/dev-f65ca154` = 该 worktree 自己的分支名（不是我建的）
- `✗` = 工作区脏。这里只有**一个** untracked 项：

```text
?? .workbuddy-ai/memory/2026-10-10.md
```

`20219` 字节，mtime `2026-10-11 00:12:55`。这是 **WorkBuddy App 自己写的会话记忆文件**（内容开头是"2026-10-10 / CG-007 Z0-D3 交接文本核验（只读，未改任何文件）"）。它没被 `.gitignore` 排除，所以显示成 untracked，于是提示符出现 `✗`。

**和我有没有关系：没有**

- 该文件 mtime 是 `00:12:55`，我的第一次提交是 `09:14:25` —— **早我九个小时**
- `find` 该目录下 `2026-10-11 00:00` 之后被改的文件，结果**只有这一个**，且是 App 写的
- 我本轮所有写操作都在 `/Volumes/D/HoTT-worktrees/cg007-w8-d3`，从未写入这个 worktree

**它为什么"落后"**

`git rev-list --left-right --count workbuddy/dev-f65ca154...dev` = `0 45`：这条分支**没有独有提交**，`dev` 比它多 **45 个**。所以它是纯滞后快照——缺 `SeqTranslateFormula.lean` 等全部 W8 文件，也没有 `devbuild_ns` 入口。

**所以刚才那个"当前目录"是怎么来的**

宿主把这个 worktree 设成了本会话的默认 cwd（`<environment_context>` 里的 `workspace_roots` 就是它），所以你在 Terminal 里一开就是这里。我的做法是：**只把它当宿主 cwd，绝不写入**；每条命令都显式 `cd /Volumes/D/HoTT-worktrees/cg007-w8-d3` 或用 `git -C <绝对路径>` 限定。

**你现在可以怎么处理**（按侵入性从小到大）

1. **什么都不做** —— 它是无害的滞后快照，那个 `✗` 只是 App 自己的记忆文件
2. **想让提示符变干净** —— 在 WorkBuddy 的 `.gitignore` 里加 `.workbuddy-ai/`（需你决定，我不擅自改）
3. **想彻底清掉这四个陈旧 worktree** —— 用 `git worktree remove`（会删 checkout，需你明确授权；`dev` 上已有它们的全部内容，不会丢东西）

我的建议是 **1 或 2**，并且继续把 CG-007 的写入放在 `/Volumes/D/HoTT-worktrees/cg007-w8-d3`。要我执行 2 或 3 的话，跟我说一声，我再动手。
